We present a reinforcement learning formulation to solve this problem in a unified framework, which is driven by a goal relative pose between the two parts. Finger coordination is shaped by a function-based auxiliary reward and regularized toward a single human reference pose, while domain randomization and a fusion of historical proprioception and object observation confer robustness to occlusion-induced estimation noise. The same recipe solves three different assembly tasks (Bottle, Syringe, and Marker). Trained purely in simulation, the policies transfer zero-shot to hardware with a single camera, demonstrating robustness to state-estimation errors caused by oc- clusion. Our experiments also reveal that in-hand assembly places demands on hand morphology and can serve as a benchmark for modern robotic hand systems. Videos and code are available at https://ltbgbird.github.io/in-hand-assembly-page/.

</details>


### [106] [Future-Aware Flow Planning for Safe UAV Target Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10166)
*Boning Feng,Haoran Zhang,Xiaowen Bi,Yanzhen Zhang,Xiaodan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向无人机目标跟随的"未来感知流规划"框架：以预测目标未来轨迹作为与规划时域对齐的残差信号引导UAV轨迹生成，并在采样循环内嵌入风险评分的可执行前缀修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有current-state跟随器在杂乱环境中具有固有预测性缺陷：转弯滞后、误选被遮挡走廊、为规避近时域不安全运动而牺牲跟踪精度；需要将目标未来预测显式耦合进规划过程并保证滤波后轨迹仍可执行。

Method: 将预测的目标未来轨迹转化为 horizon-aligned 残差信号引导干净的UAV轨迹生成；在采样循环内嵌入风险评分的可执行前缀修复（executable-prefix repair）；在固定ID/OOD滚动时域基准上与Future-MPC等对比，并做未来自适应与安全修复的消融，以及接口/感知/控制器执行层面的压力测试。

Result: 非全面碾压式提升：ID碰撞率为零且ID safe-tracking time最高；OOD macro碰撞率与最终跟踪误差最低；但Future-MPC在平滑性及部分阈值化OOD成功率指标上更优。消融显示未来自适应先改善候选生成、再由安全修复保障可行。

Conclusion: horizon-aligned未来自适应与嵌入式前缀修复是杂乱环境下安全UAV目标跟随的互补要素；结论限于所测试的仿真条件，缺乏真机验证。

Abstract: UAV target following in cluttered environments is inherently predictive: current-state followers can lag behind turns, choose blocked corridors, or trade tracking for unsafe near-horizon motion. We propose a future-aware flow planning framework for state-informed UAV target following. Predicted target futures guide clean UAV trajectory generation as horizon-aligned residual signals, while risk-scored executable-prefix repair is embedded inside the sampling loop. On fixed ID/OOD receding-horizon benchmarks, the planner improves the intended safety--tracking trade-off rather than dominating every metric: it matches zero measured ID collision rate with the highest ID safe-tracking time, and gives the lowest OOD macro collision rate and final tracking error among the displayed methods, while Future-MPC remains smoother and stronger on some thresholded OOD success metrics under its hand-designed objective. Ablations show that future adaptation improves candidate generation before safety repair, and simulator-facing stress tests probe interface, sensing, and controller-execution effects. These results support horizon-aligned future adaptation and embedded prefix repair as complementary ingredients for safe UAV target following under the tested simulation conditions.

</details>


### [107] [Multi-Robot Scanner for Automated Full-Body Dermoscopic Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10169)
*Valerio Franchi,Rafael Garcia,Nuno Gracias,Ricard Campos,Josep Quintana,Sandra González-Villà,Mark Ventura,Nuria Ferrera,Clément Lenoir,Josep Malvehy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 构建由四台UR10机械臂末端相机组成的全身皮肤成像扫描系统，具备视角规划、多臂协同控制与碰撞检测，实现无接触的皮肤镜级自动成像，并与接触式皮肤镜及Vectra系统进行对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身摄影（TBP）分辨率不足，无法看清黑色素瘤等皮损的皮肤镜级细节；而手持皮肤镜需逐点手动检查、效率低且依赖接触。作者希望以多机械臂+末端相机自动采集皮肤镜质量图像，弥合全身摄影与手持皮肤镜之间的差距，并融入筛查流程。

Method: 硬件上采用四台UR10机械臂，末端搭载相机对全身皮损成像；算法上包含视角规划算法（为每个痣选择最佳相机位姿）、高层控制器（使多臂同时工作并避免互扰）与碰撞检测器（接触人或物体时立即停止）。评估采用真实患者全身扫描，将所得图像与接触式皮肤镜和Vectra全身摄影系统在临床相关皮损特征上对比，并使用USAF 1951分辨率靶量化真实光学分辨能力。

Result: 本系统最小可分辨特征尺寸为22.1微米，接触式皮肤镜为8.8微米；在大多数临床相关特征上，扫描仪持续优于Vectra，并在多数评估特征上达到与接触式皮肤镜相当的表现。

Conclusion: 该系统可自动、无接触地获取皮肤镜质量图像，无需单独的手持皮肤镜检查，缩小了全身摄影与手持皮肤镜之间的性能差距，具备未来整合进筛查工作流的潜力。

Abstract: This paper outlines the specifications and design approach used to construct a full body imaging scanner capable of capturing skin lesions at a dermatoscopic level using cameras mounted on the end-effectors of four UR10 manipulators. The system possesses a view-planning algorithm capable of appropriately selecting the best camera position to acquire images of moles, a high-level controller to allow the manipulators to work simultaneously and a collision-detector that halts the manipulators when they make contact with an object or a person. We evaluate the system through real-patient full-body scans, comparing acquired images against contact dermoscopy and an existing total-body photography system (Vectra) across clinically relevant lesion features, and quantify true optical resolving power using a USAF 1951 resolution target, yielding a smallest resolvable feature size of 22.1 microns for our scanner compared to 8.8 microns for contact dermoscopy. Results show the scanner consistently outperforms Vectra across most clinically relevant features and achieves comparable performance to contact dermoscopy for the majority of features assessed. By acquiring dermatoscopic-quality images automatically and without contact, and without requiring a separate manual dermoscopic examination, the scanner closes part of the gap between total-body photography and handheld dermoscopy, suggesting potential for future integration into screening workflows.

</details>


### [108] [Adaptive Shared Control with Online Bounded-Rational Human Behavior Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10215)
*Henry Ascencio Trejo,Roel Pieters,Gokhan Alcan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向非线性控制仿射系统的自适应共享人机控制框架，将人类建模为level-k有限理性，用ADP离线构建候选策略库，在线通过状态转移残差+遗忘因子估计人类行为概率分布，并计算分布感知的单步最优响应（期望人类输入下有闭式解），在基准非线性系统镇定与平面机械臂共享控制仿真中降低KL散度与累计代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共享控制多假设人类完全理性，与真实人类的有界理性行为不符，导致机器人辅助策略次优；同时需要一种能在线辨识人类意图、而非单一策略选择或简单加权的方法。

Method: 1) 用level-k有界理性博弈模型，通过交替最优响应生成有限候选人-机策略库，价值函数与策略由自适应动态规划(ADP)近似；2) 在线用状态转移残差与候选人类策略预测轨迹比较，经遗忘因子累积，映射为候选库上的概率人类行为模型；3) 机器人不选单点策略也不加权平均，而是对完整估计分布最小化期望协作代价，求分布感知单步最优响应；4) 二次终端价值近似+欧拉状态传播下，该响应具有以期望人类输入表示的闭式解。

Result: 在非线性系统镇定基准与平面机械臂共享控制仿真中，估计人类行为分布与仿真真实分布的KL散度持续下降；共享控制交互期机器人的累计运行代价低于"最大概率策略"与"概率加权"两种基线。

Conclusion: 将有限理性人类建模、ADP候选策略库与分布感知最优响应结合，可在线适应真实人类行为并提升共享控制的协作性能，且具备闭式可实现性；当前验证限于仿真。

Abstract: This work considers adaptive shared human-robot control for nonlinear control-affine systems, where the assumption of a fully rational human is relaxed and the robot adapts its assistance to observed boundedly rational human behavior. We use a level-k bounded-rationality model of the two-player game to construct a finite bank of candidate human and robot policies through alternating best-response computations, with the associated value functions and policies approximated using adaptive dynamic programming. During the shared-control interaction, state-transition residuals compare the measured system evolution with the trajectories predicted by the candidate human policies. The residuals are accumulated using a forgetting factor and mapped to a probabilistic human-behavior model over the finite candidate bank. Rather than selecting a single candidate or averaging stored robot policies, the robot computes a distribution-aware one-step best response by minimizing an expected cooperative cost over the complete estimated human behavior distribution. For a quadratic terminal-value approximation and Euler state propagation, this response admits a closed-form solution expressed in terms of the expected human input. The proposed methods are evaluated in simulations of a benchmark nonlinear system stabilization task, and of a planar manipulator shared control setup. The reported results show decreasing Kullback-Leibler divergence between the estimated and simulated human behavior distributions, and a lower accumulated running cost for the robot agent over the shared control interaction period, than the maximum-probability and probability-weighted alternative policies baseline.

</details>


### [109] [CougarTail & CUB: A General-Purpose Mast and Central Utility Board for Cylindrical Underwater Enclosures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10230)
*Ben Washburn,Clayton Smith,Eli Gaskin,Brighton Anderson,Braden Meyers,Brady Moon,Joshua Mangelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CUB与CougarTail：一种适配4英寸圆柱水密舱的圆形PCB电子集成方案及其开源传感桅杆，将CougUV的电子堆叠从200 mm/709 g压缩至25 mm/156 g。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下圆柱形水密舱内普遍堆叠矩形PCB，对圆形截面体积极度低效，浪费了宝贵的载荷容量与浮力配平预算。作者希望为该设计缺口提供通用解决方案。

Method: CUB是一块为4英寸直径舱体设计的圆形PCB，集成Raspberry Pi Compute Module 5与STM32微控制器，并提供电源管理与辅助接口；以同轴方式安装。CougarTail是开源配套传感桅杆，容纳GPS天线与两根双频（2.4/5 GHz）全向PCB天线。二者均通过台架测试并在CougUV平台上完成集成验证。

Result: 在CougUV上将电子堆叠长度从200 mm降至25 mm，质量从709 g降至156 g，释放的长度与质量预算回流至载荷与浮力配平；CUB与CougarTail均通过台架测试及整机集成验证。

Conclusion: 圆形同轴PCB集成是一种可推广的水下系统电子堆叠紧凑化路径，可显著改善圆柱舱内的空间与浮力管理，相关设计开源。

Abstract: Cylindrical watertight enclosures are widely used across various underwater systems, from unmanned underwater vehicles (UUVs), to remotely operated vehicles (ROVs), to various sensor platforms. However, electronics are typically built on rectangular PCBs arranged in horizontal stacks, which inefficiently occupy the circular cross-section volume that is critical for both payload capacity and buoyancy management. This paper presents CUB (Central Utility Board) and CougarTail, a general-purpose system designed to address this gap. CUB is a circular PCB sized for 4-inch-diameter enclosures that consolidates a Raspberry Pi Compute Module 5 (CM5) and an STM32 microcontroller, while also providing power management features and auxiliary connections. Mounted coaxially, CUB reduces the electronics stack of our CougUV from 200 mm of tube length and 709 g to 25 mm and 156 g, returning that length and mass budget to payload and buoyancy trim. CougarTail is an open-source companion sensor mast that houses a GPS antenna and two dual-band (2.4 and 5 GHz) omnidirectional PCB antennas. Both components are validated through bench testing and integration on a CougUV platform, our small open-sourced torpedo UUVs.

</details>


### [110] [FolDeX: A Physical-World Benchmark for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation of Deformable Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10243)
*Chenhuan Liu,Yi Xu,Feng Wu,Hanyang Wang,Wenxiao Kuai,Weihao Ding,Shan Wang,Yang Liu,Shuyong Gao,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FolDeX 是一个完全基于真实机器人数据构建的物理世界基准，以衣物折叠为主要任务，围绕四条研究轴（部署期人类干预/恢复数据、跨任务迁移、跨场景复用、跨本体迁移）研究异构真实机器人经验的高效复用，提供 2000+ 小时真实数据、20+ 任务、10+ 本体，并建立公平的公开评测平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身 AI（含 VLA 与世界-动作模型）在仿真中表现良好，但迁移到真实机器人时性能常显著下降，尤其是长时程可变形物体操作中，策略需跟踪变化状态并完成可靠的多阶段双臂交互。现有真实机器人基准多局限于短时程刚体任务，对长时程可变形操作覆盖不足；同时真实机器人数据采集成本高昂，如何高效复用异构物理经验缺乏统一研究载体。

Method: 提出 FolDeX 基准，全部由真实机器人数据构成，主任务为衣物折叠。按四条研究轴组织：1）利用部署过程中采集的人类干预与恢复数据；2）跨任务迁移数据，包括跨衣物类别以及从刚体到可变形物体操作；3）跨场景复用数据，覆盖光照、背景与布局变化；4）跨机器人本体迁移数据。构建包含 2000+ 小时真实机器人数据、20+ 任务、10+ 本体的数据集，并搭建面向外部提交策略的标准化真实机器人评测平台，含统一任务、留出物理物体、受控初始化与统一执行协议。

Result: 构建了 FolDeX 基准与公开评测平台（https://ai.midea.com/#/fold-challenge），提供 2000+ 小时真实机器人数据，覆盖 20+ 任务与 10+ 本体；建立了具备标准化任务、留出物理物体、受控初始化与统一执行协议的真实机器人评测流程。

Conclusion: FolDeX 有望成为异构真实机器人数据复用与可靠长时程可变形操作研究的统一测试平台。

Abstract: Embodied AI, including vision-language-action and world-action models, must operate reliably in the physical world. Yet methods that perform well in simulation can degrade substantially on real robots, especially in long-horizon deformable-object manipulation, where policies must track changing states and execute reliable multi-stage bimanual interactions. Existing real-robot benchmarks mainly focus on short-horizon rigid-object tasks and offer limited coverage of long-horizon deformable manipulation. We introduce FolDeX, a physical-world benchmark built entirely from real-robot data, with garment folding as its primary task. Since real-robot data collection is costly, FolDeX studies how heterogeneous physical experience can be reused efficiently. The benchmark is organized around four research axes: leveraging human intervention and recovery data collected during deployment; transferring data across tasks, including across garment categories and from rigid to deformable-object manipulation; reusing data across scenes with changes in lighting, background, and layout; and transferring data across robotic embodiments. FolDeX provides 2,000+ hours of real-robot data spanning 20+ tasks and 10+ embodiments. We also establish a fair real-robot evaluation platform for externally submitted policies, with standardized tasks, held-out physical objects, controlled initializations, and a unified execution protocol. The platform is publicly accessible at https://ai.midea.com/#/fold-challenge. We hope FolDeX will serve as a unified testbed for heterogeneous real-robot data reuse and reliable long-horizon deformable manipulation.

</details>


### [111] [SwingBot: Learning Whole-Body Brachiation for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10283)
*Yujie Xiong,Peng Zhai,Taixian Hou,Quancheng Qian,Cunwang Liu,Kangmai Hu,Long Yang,Zhiyan Dong,Lihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SwingBot 学习框架，使高自由度人形机器人能够借助被动腕钩完成连续的臂行（brachiation）移动，并在真实硬件上实现连续横杆穿越及对负载、扰动和杆距变化的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面路径受阻时，灵长类通过臂行在头顶支撑物间移动，这为机器人在杂乱或危险环境中的移动提供了互补的运动模式。但将这一能力赋予高自由度人形机器人很困难：控制器需自行发现长时程的"释放—摆动—抓取"序列，协调左右交替接触与全身动量，且缺乏可靠的段间相对位移与钩子接触状态测量。

Method: 围绕臂行任务结构组织学习：1）以仿生关键帧引导初期探索，使稀疏的"释放—摆动—抓取"转变得以被采样到；2）采用循环的 privileged-state 估计，为部署阶段提供紧凑的位置与接触隐变量，从而在无直接测量条件下完成控制。

Result: 硬件实验展示了连续横杆穿越能力，并对负载变化、外部扰动和不同横杆间距表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该公式化为全身机器人臂行提供了一条切实可行的路径。

Abstract: Brachiation enables primates to move across overhead supports when ground paths are blocked, suggesting a complementary locomotion mode for robots operating in cluttered or hazardous environments. Bringing this capabil?ity to high-DoF humanoid robots is difficult because the controller must discover a long-horizon release-swing-capture sequence, coordinate alternating contacts with whole-body momentum, and act without reliable measurements of segment?relative displacement or hook-contact state. We present SwingBot, a learning framework for continuous humanoid brachiation with passive wrist hooks. Swing?Bot makes the task trainable by organizing learning around the structure of brachi?ation: biomimetic keyframes make rare release-swing-capture transitions reach?able during early exploration, and recurrent privileged-state estimation provides compact position and contact latents for deployment. Hardware experiments demonstrate continuous bar traversal and robustness to payload, external distur?bances and different bar spacings, showing that this formulation offers a practical route to whole-body robotic brachiation.

</details>


### [112] [Learning Terrain-Adaptive Humanoid Locomotion on Granular Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10286)
*Junnosuke Kamohara,Feiyang Wu,Andy Ningan Zong,Daniel I. Goldman,Yashwanth Nakka,Seth Hutchinson,Ye Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于三维阻力理论(3D RFT)的物理颗粒接触模型，用于人形机器人在颗粒地形上的强化学习训练，并结合教师-学生RL与VAE地形编码实现地形自适应控制，在仿真与玄武岩、干沙、海滩沙等真实颗粒地形上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒地形的足-地交互动力学复杂且难以建模，现有方法要么忽略颗粒接触动力学，要么仅用简化的法向力模型加启发式切向分量，导致策略难以迁移到真实颗粒地形。

Method: 1) 基于三维阻力理论(3D RFT)构建物理接地(physics-grounded)的颗粒接触模型，无需启发式即可求解颗粒侵入动力学，捕捉真实穿透与切向阻力；2) 用该求解器高效仿真颗粒地形用于RL训练；3) 通过教师-学生RL训练地形自适应运动控制器，用变分自编码器(VAE)将地形信息编码为紧凑隐表示。

Result: 基于MPM与NVIDIA Newton的仿真研究表明：方法可泛化到未见过的颗粒地形，成功率显著高于基线，并实现零样本地形识别与自适应；在玄武岩、干沙、海滩沙等多样化真实颗粒地形上完成大量硬件实验验证。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首次在真实世界颗粒地形上实现敏捷人形机器人运动；该接触求解器与地形自适应框架可弥合颗粒地形上刚体接触模型失效的sim-to-real差距。

Abstract: Humanoid locomotion on granular terrain remains a significant challenge due to its complex foot-terrain interaction dynamics that are difficult to model. Existing approaches either ignore granular contact dynamics or incorporate simplified normal force models with heuristic tangential components. In this work, we present a physics-grounded granular contact model based on three-dimensional resistive force theory (3D RFT) and efficiently simulate granular terrain for reinforcement learning (RL) training. Unlike traditional rigid contact models and simplified granular contact models with ad-hoc heuristics, our contact solver produces physically accurate granular intrusion dynamics without resorting to heuristics. It captures realistic penetration and tangential drag during training, enabling the policy to learn behaviors that transfer reliably to real-world granular terrain where rigid contact models fail. To adapt to varying terrain conditions, we train a terrain-adaptive locomotion controller via teacher-student RL, using a variational autoencoder to encode terrain information into a compact latent representation. Simulation studies using material point method (MPM) with NVIDIA Newton demonstrate that our method generalizes to unseen granular terrains, achieves a significantly higher success rate than baselines, and demonstrates zero-shot terrain identification and adaptation. We further validate our approach through extensive hardware experiments across diverse real-world granular terrains including basalt, dry sand, and beach sand. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of agile humanoid locomotion on real-world granular terrain. Project page: https://humanoid-gm-locomotion.github.io/HUMANOID-GM/

</details>


### [113] [Deformable Object Manipulation under Partial Observability via Real-Time Full-Shape Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10308)
*Kosar Behnia,Ville Kyrki,Gokhan Alcan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出轻量级条件循环变分自编码器 cRVAE，仅用角点观测即可推断可变形物体全状态，作为滚动时域最优控制的前向模型，实现避障协同操控，速度比 XPBD 快数百至千倍，并在 Unitree Go2 上部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形物体操控面临高维状态空间、欠驱动动力学与部分可观测性的三重挑战。现有物理模型（如参数辨识的 XPBD）需要物理参数输入并做在线辨识，计算开销大，难以满足实时控制预算，且难以仅凭有限的角点传感获取全形状状态。

Method: cRVAE：轻量级条件循环变分自编码器，以循环结构建模时序，用角点节点观测作为条件输入，推断可变形物体完整状态；训练后作为前向模型嵌入滚动时域（receding-horizon）最优控制框架，用于障碍感知的协同操控，推理阶段不使用任何物理参数、不做在线参数辨识。

Result: 在绳与布料仿真中，cRVAE 仅凭角点测量即可估计全状态，精度与参数辨识的 XPBD 模型相当；单次前向推理在绳上约快 350 倍、布料上超 1500 倍，使时域规划保持在 100 ms 控制预算内（XPBD 在短时域即已超时）；并在 Unitree Go2 机器人上完成硬件部署演示。

Conclusion: 从角点传感实时估计全形状状态是实现硬件部署的关键，cRVAE 兼具精度与速度优势，为可变形物体实时协同操控提供了可行路径，但由于验证主要限于仿真与初步真机演示，泛化至更复杂物体与真实接触场景仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Manipulating deformable objects (DOs) is challenging due to their high-dimensional state space, underactuated dynamics, and partial observability. In this paper, we propose cRVAE, a lightweight conditional recurrent variational autoencoder that estimates the full DO state from only partial corner-node observations during inference. The resulting model is used as the forward model in a receding-horizon optimal control framework for obstacle-aware collaborative DO manipulation. In simulation on rope and fabric, cRVAE estimates the full DO state from the available corner-node measurements alone, matching the accuracy of a parameter-identified XPBD model. At inference it uses no physical parameters as model inputs and performs no online parameter identification. It also runs approximately 350 times faster on the rope and over 1500 times faster on the fabric per forward pass, keeping horizon-based planning within the 100 ms control budget where XPBD exceeds it already at short horizons. Full-shape estimation from corner sensing at in-loop speed is what makes the model deployable on hardware, which we demonstrate on a Unitree Go2 robot.

</details>


### [114] [Odometer-Agnostic Drift Correction Using OpenStreetMap Lane Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10336)
*Joaquin Caballero,Emilio Garcia-Fidalgo,Alberto Ortiz,Jarno Ralli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量、开源的里程计无关漂移校正方法，通过将短轨迹段与OSM车道中心线直接对齐来消除长期漂移，无需稠密地图或复杂匹配流程，在LiDAR与视觉里程计后端上均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增量位姿积分导致长期漂移，在大尺度或无回环环境中尤为严重；现有地图辅助方法依赖稠密地图、传感器专用处理或复杂匹配管线，代价高、泛化差。

Method: 将漂移校正建模为近期里程计短轨迹段与稀疏OSM车道中心线几何之间的直接对齐问题，采用轻量开源、与里程计后端无关的框架，在无稠密先验与昂贵预处理条件下实现在线运行。

Result: 在LiDAR与视觉里程计后端实验中均获得一致性提升，在严重漂移场景下增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 稀疏地图先验（OSM车道线）足以支撑高效在线漂移校正，方法里程计无关、轻量且开源，为无回环大尺度定位提供低成本解决方案。

Abstract: Despite significant progress in odometry estimation, long-term drift remains a fundamental limitation of incremental pose integration, especially in large-scale or loop-free environments. Existing map-assisted methods can reduce drift, but often depend on dense maps, sensor-specific processing, or complex matching pipelines. We propose a lightweight open-source, odometry-agnostic correction method that aligns short trajectory segments to OpenStreetMap (OSM) lane centerlines. By formulating drift correction as a direct alignment between recent odometry and sparse lane geometry, the method enables efficient online operation without dense priors or expensive preprocessing. Experiments with LiDAR and visual odometry backends demonstrate consistent improvements, with particularly strong gains under severe drift.

</details>


### [115] [A Confidence-Aware Multimodal Fusion Framework for Industrial Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10339)
*Xinyu Liu,Qiqi Dong,Boya Jia,Yi Zhang,Binbin Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出置信度感知多模态融合框架（CAMF），融合物体6D位姿、注视、骨架运动与IMU手部运动四种模态，通过置信度趋势驱动的动态融合机制与置信度引导的平衡学习策略，实现工业人机协作中可靠的人体意图预测，准确率91.86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业人机协作（HRC）中机器人需提前、可靠地预测人类意图，才能主动配合并保障安全；但单一模态信息不足，且不同模态在低光照、遮挡等条件下可靠性剧烈波动，固定权重的多模态融合会引入噪声并产生跨模态学习偏置，导致预测不稳定。

Method: CAMF框架融合四类异构模态（物体6D位姿、眼动注视、骨架运动、IMU手部运动）。核心包含三部分：（1）置信度趋势驱动的动态融合机制，嵌入BiLSTM，按实时模态可靠性自适应平衡双向时序特征；（2）置信度引导的平衡学习策略，配合置信度冻结机制动态调整网络梯度、抑制低质量模态噪声；（3）基于UR3协作机器人搭建物理实验平台进行验证。

Result: 意图识别准确率达91.86%，在整体性能与稳定性上优于现有多模态融合方法；在低光照和部分遮挡干扰下仍保持满意精度；在实际装配任务中实现主动、稳定的人机协作，具有较强环境适应性。

Conclusion: 置信度建模是提升工业人机协作用意预测鲁棒性的有效途径，动态融合与平衡学习可缓解模态质量波动与跨模态偏置，框架具备实际部署的环境适应性，但跨任务、跨场景泛化性仍待进一步验证。

Abstract: A confidence-aware multimodal fusion framework (CAMF) is proposed to realize reliable human intention prediction for industrial human-robot collaboration. This framework fuses four heterogeneous modalities including object 6D pose, gaze, skeletal motion and IMU-based hand motion. It embeds a confidence-trend-driven dynamic fusion mechanism into BiLSTM to adaptively balance bidirectional temporal features according to real-time modality reliability. A confidence-guided balanced learning strategy combined with a confidence freezing mechanism is further adopted to adjust network gradients dynamically, suppress noise from low-quality modalities and mitigate cross-modal learning bias. A physical platform based on the UR3 collaborative robot is built for experimental validation. Comparative results show that the proposed method reaches an intention recognition accuracy of 91.86% and outperforms existing multimodal fusion approaches in overall performance and stability. It also maintains satisfactory accuracy under low light and partial occlusion interference. In practical assembly tasks, the framework enables proactive and stable human-robot cooperation with strong environmental adaptability.

</details>


### [116] [Data-Driven Risk Fields for Safer End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10377)
*Yuanxin Tian,Zhiyuan Liu,Jinhao Li,Zhenhua Xu,Wenhao Yu,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRiF：用成对相对风险排序标签替代手工绝对风险分数回归，学习共享BEV特征的动态风险场，并联合静态地图分割与车辆规划，在Bench2Drive上提升驾驶分数、成功率与碰撞相关指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶缺乏显式风险感知能力。规则式风险模型可解释但依赖手工函数、系数与阈值；学习式风险表示虽减少人工设计，但监督多来自占用栅格标签或启发式代价值，未必反映自车条件下的规划风险。

Method: 提出数据驱动的风险场框架DRiF：共享BEV特征同时服务静态地图分割、动态风险预测与车辆规划；将规则式安全先验转化为成对风险标签，训练风险场保持相对风险序关系，而非回归手工绝对分数。

Result: Bench2Drive上整体性能具竞争力，驾驶分数、成功率及碰撞相关指标一致性提升。

Conclusion: 相对风险监督可将显式安全结构与端到端规划有效衔接；数据与代码将公开。

Abstract: Safety is a fundamental requirement for autonomous driving, yet existing end-to-end driving models still lack explicit risk-aware learning capacities. Existing rule-based risk models provide interpretable safety priors, yet their absolute risk scores depend on handcrafted functions, coefficients, and thresholds. Learning-based risk representations reduce part of this manual design, but their supervision often relies on occupancy-derived labels or heuristic cost values, which may not capture ego-conditioned planning risk. In this paper, we propose DRiF, a data-driven risk-field framework for safer end-to-end autonomous driving. DRiF learns a shared BEV feature with static map segmentation, dynamic risk prediction, and vehicle planning. For dynamic risk learning, DRiF converts rule-based safety priors into pairwise risk labels, and trains the risk field to preserve relative risk ordering instead of regressing handcrafted absolute scores. Experiments on Bench2Drive show that DRiF achieves competitive overall performance, with consistent improvements in driving score, success rate, and collision-related metrics. These results establish relative risk supervision as an effective way to connect explicit safety structure with end-to-end planning. The data and code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [117] [A traffic management system for large and heterogeneous vehicles in narrow industrial environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10400)
*Alessandro Bonetti,Silvia Proia,Simone Guidetti,Lorenzo Sabattini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于NURBS路网与L-MAPF算法的AGV交通管理系统，结合改进的有界视野CBS与滚动视野冲突消解策略，实现自适应时间范围、死锁检测与执行层安全交互，在真实工业场景中吞吐量较规则法/SOTA/优先级L-MAPF提升最高11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Logistics 4.0中高密度工业环境下AGV协同是关键难题：传统交通管理依赖基于协商的优先级分配，效率低；现有AGV车队调度方法多针对网格化、标准化的理想环境，难以适配非标准化、窄双向走廊、高交通密度且车辆异构的真实工业现场。

Method: 以NURBS曲线生成非网格化路网，构建基于L-MAPF的交通管理系统；将改进的有界视野冲突搜索（CBS）嵌入滚动视野冲突消解（RHCR）框架，为每个智能体扩展时间视野以在拓扑图识别的走廊内有效消解冲突。系统含三个关键组件：带自适应时间范围调节的anytime冲突消解策略、与真实AGV安全且符合标准交互的执行层、以及死锁检测与消解机制。

Result: 在真实工业环境实验中实现更高吞吐量，相比传统规则式交通管理系统、一种SOTA工业方法及基于优先级的L-MAPF变体，提升最高达11%，并保持持续运行与效率改善。

Conclusion: 该工作表明基于NURBS路网与滚动视野CBS的L-MAPF框架可有效支撑非标准化工业场景下异构AGV车队的局部最优协同，兼顾安全性、死锁消解与工厂级部署可行性。

Abstract: The coordination of Automated Guided Vehicles (AGVs) in high-density industrial environments represents a critical challenge within Logistics 4.0, as traditional traffic management methods often lead to inefficiencies caused by negotiation-based priority assignment. To overcome the resulting limitations, this paper presents an innovative AGV traffic management system based on a Lifelong Multi-Agent Path Finding (L-MAPF) algorithm operating on roadmaps generated with Non-Uniform Rational B-Splines (NURBS) curves. The approach guarantees locally optimal coordination and ensures safe operation of large and heterogeneous AGVs. Building on this concept, the proposed framework integrates a modified version of the Bounded Horizon Conflict Based Search (CBS) technique within a Rolling Horizon Conflict Resolution strategy, utilizing an extended time horizon for each agent to enable effective conflict resolution in corridors identified by a topological map. In contrast to state-of-the-art methods for AGV fleet traffic management, the proposed solution is designed for real-world, non-standardized (i.e., non-grid-like) industrial settings characterized by narrow bidirectional corridors and high-traffic density, where AGVs of various sizes and capabilities operate simultaneously. Key contributions include an anytime conflict resolution strategy with adaptive time horizon regulation, an execution layer for safe and standard-compliant interaction with real AGVs, and an advanced mechanism for deadlock detection and resolution. Experimental results obtained in realistic industrial environments demonstrate higher throughput, with improvements of up to 11% over a conventional rule-based traffic management system, a state-of-the-art industrial method, and a priority-based L-MAPF variant, while maintaining continuous operation and improved efficiency.

</details>


### [118] [Frequency-Conditioned Flow Matching for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10405)
*Haochen Niu,Shengye Dong,Hao Liu,Peiwen Lin,Wang Chuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FreqFM 提出频域条件化的 Flow Matching 框架，将动作频率从隐式轨迹属性提升为显式条件维度，贯穿生成全过程，在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、VLA-Arena 及六项真机任务上稳定提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 Flow Matching 的 VLA 模型通常在时域坐标生成动作，而机器人动作本质是频率分量高度非均匀分布的时间相关轨迹，现有方法未显式建模或系统利用这一频率异质性。

Method: 在 DCT 频域坐标下构建频谱匹配的源分布，跨频率自适应平衡训练目标，并用对应参考传输尺度约束逐频引导残差；可即插即用地集成到现有 Flow Matching 动作专家中，不改变 VLA 主干。

Result: LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、VLA-Arena 上性能一致提升，LIBERO-Plus 提升 9.3 个点；六项真机任务进一步验证有效性。

Conclusion: 频域条件化是提升 VLA 动作生成的通用且低侵入手段，具备仿真与真机双重验证。

Abstract: Robot actions are temporally correlated trajectories whose frequency components encode motion at different scales with highly non-uniform energy distributions. Yet Flow Matching--based vision-language-action (VLA) models typically generate actions in temporal coordinates, without explicitly modeling or systematically leveraging this frequency heterogeneity. We introduce \emph{FreqFM}, a frequency-conditioned Flow Matching framework for VLA models. It raises action frequency from an implicit trajectory property to an explicit conditioning dimension that spans the entire generation pipeline. Concretely, in DCT frequency coordinates, FreqFM constructs a spectrum-matched source distribution, adaptively balances the objective across frequencies, and constrains per-frequency guidance residuals using the corresponding reference transport scales. FreqFM integrates into existing Flow Matching action experts without changing the VLA backbone. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and VLA-Arena, FreqFM consistently improves performance, including a 9.3-point gain on LIBERO-Plus, and further demonstrates its effectiveness on six real-robot tasks.

</details>


### [119] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous UAV Exploration in Wildfire Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10433)
*Caden Chandra,Jerry Ng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究构建了深度强化学习（DRL）框架，训练无人机（UAV）智能体在模拟野火环境中进行导航与监测，观察到损失收敛、奖励提升以及沿火线边界跟踪等稳定行为，表明DRL可用于自主野火监测，且环境结构与奖励设计影响策略效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火监测任务危险且覆盖范围大，人工或传统方法成本高、风险高，因此探索用DRL驱动的无人机实现自主导航与监测。

Method: 在仿真野火环境中搭建DRL训练框架，让UAV智能体通过试错学习导航与监测策略，并观察损失曲线、奖励信号与导航轨迹（如火场边界跟踪）的演化。

Result: 随训练进行，损失趋势收敛、奖励改善、导航模式更一致（如出现火线边界跟踪行为）；结果表明环境结构与奖励设计对策略有效性有明显影响。

Conclusion: DRL在无人机自主野火监测方面具有潜力，但结论主要建立在仿真环境上，尚缺真实部署与定量基准，奖励与环境设计是后续关键改进方向。

Abstract: This study develops a deep reinforcement learning framework for training Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) agents to navigate and monitor simulated wildfire environments. Results show that agents learn increasingly stable and effective behaviors over time, as demonstrated by converging loss trends, improved reward signals, and more consistent navigation patterns such as fire-boundary tracking. Overall, these findings highlight the potential of deep reinforcement learning (DRL) based UAV systems for autonomous wildfire monitoring and suggest that environmental structure and reward design influence policy effectiveness.

</details>


### [120] [Coastal Environment Generation with HoloOcean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10484)
*Abigail Austin,Brady Moon,Joshua G. Mangelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于 Unreal Engine 5 的自动化海岸环境生成流水线，仅需一张俯视海岸图像即可为 HoloOcean 海洋机器人仿真器生成高程图并自动选择与布置资产，降低仿真环境构建门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋机器人（UUV/USV）自主与感知算法的开发测试依赖仿真，成本低且安全，但仿真环境资源稀缺；现有海洋仿真套件仅支持手动编辑或创建场景，需要已有数据或对环境系统的专业知识，导致环境构建成为瓶颈。

Method: 构建一条 Unreal Engine 5 关卡生成流水线，面向 HoloOcean 仿真器：以用户提供的俯视海岸图像为输入，从图像中生成高程图（height map）数据，并自动完成资产的选取与在环境中的摆放，实现海岸场景的端到端自动生成。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果；声称该流水线可由单张俯视图像自动生成可用海岸仿真环境，替代原有手动编辑流程，减少对既有数据与环境系统专业知识的依赖。

Conclusion: 该工作为海洋机器人仿真提供了一种低门槛、自动化的环境生成方案，输入仅为图像，可直接服务于 UUV/USV 算法的开发与验证。

Abstract: Marine robotic simulation provides a safe and inexpensive method of developing and testing algorithms for unmanned underwater vehicle (UUV) and unmanned surface vessel (USV) autonomy and perception before full field deployment. However, these simulations are often limited by the availability of simulated environments. Current marine robotics simulation suites offer manual ways to edit or create environments, but they require existing data or specialized knowledge of the environment system. To address these issues, we introduce a novel Unreal Engine 5 level generation pipeline that enables automatic creation of coastal environments for HoloOcean. Our pipeline relies on a user-provided overhead image of a coastal scene. The pipeline then uses the image to generate height map data, as well as automatically select assets and place them in the environment.

</details>


### [121] [DUET-DINO: Simultaneous Cross-View World Modeling for Latent Planning in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10506)
*Nisarga Nilavadi,Ralf Römer,Moritz Reuss,Michael Krawez,Tobias Jülg,Angela P. Schoellig,Rudolf Lioutikov,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DUET-DINO：跨视角同步潜在世界模型，用侧视+腕部相机联合动作条件预测，实现全7-DoF末端执行器零样本目标条件规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件潜在世界模型在做细粒度空间与旋转动作预测时不可靠，无法支撑完整7-DoF末端控制；单视角或独立双视角建模无法兼顾全局场景与夹爪局部信息。

Method: 提出DUET-DINO，同时学习静态侧视与腕部相机观测的动作条件潜在预测，通过跨视角条件注入实现互补表征融合，从而在完整7-DoF动作空间上进行潜在规划；在DROID与RoboArena上从零训练，并使用DINOv3/ V-JEPA 2进行表征对比。

Result: 在reach、angled-reach、multi-goal grasp-and-lift任务上分别达92%、72.5%、60.0%，持续优于单视角和独立双视角基线；在视觉分布偏移下稳健泛化；DINOv3较V-JEPA 2腕部预测更能捕捉细粒度动作引发的场景变化。

Conclusion: 跨视角同步潜在世界模型能有效提升细粒度7-DoF操作规划性能，表征选择对下游规划影响显著；代码与权重将开源。

Abstract: Action-conditioned latent world models predict future visual representations, enabling zero-shot goal-conditioned robot planning and control. However, their predictions for fine-grained spatial and rotational actions are unreliable for full 7-DoF end-effector control. To address this gap, we introduce DUET-DINO, a simultaneous cross-view latent world model that jointly learns action-conditioned predictions from static side- and wrist-camera observations through cross-view conditioning. By exploiting complementary global scene and gripper-centric information, DUET-DINO enables latent planning over the full 7-DoF action space. Across spatially diverse reach, orientation-intensive angled-reach, and multi-goal grasp-and-lift tasks, DUET-DINO consistently outperforms single-view and independent dual-view baselines, achieving 92% success on reach, 72.5% on angled-reach, and 60.0% on lift tasks. DUET-DINO is trained from scratch on DROID and RoboArena datasets and generalizes robustly under visual distribution shifts. We further show that while V-JEPA 2 wrist-view predictions underestimate visual dynamics induced by fine-grained actions, DINOv3 predictions better capture action-conditioned scene changes, leading to stronger downstream planning. The code and model checkpoints will be open-sourced. Project page: https://utn-air.github.io/DUET-DINO

</details>


### [122] [Show-Harness: Just a VLM Agent Can Play Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10522)
*Yanzhe Chen,Zechen Bai,Zhijun Cao,Wenzheng Zeng,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Yiqi Lin,Guoqiang Liang,Kevin Yuchen Ma,Qiming Huang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Show-Harness 提出一个"具身马鞍"式语义接口，将 VLM 的意图映射为离散语义动作单元，再由具体本体解释器确定性落到机器人动作，实现闭源前沿 VLM 零样本控制与开源小模型低成本微调；并扩展到 GUI 示教采集接口 GUMI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础视觉语言模型具备广泛世界知识，但将这种智能转化为可执行的机器人控制仍困难：端到端 VLA 需要昂贵的本体特定预训练与模型容量，而直接让 VLM 输出底层控制信号又超出其能力粒度。作者希望找到一个"正确接口"，在不增加模型容量、不做本体特定预训练的前提下释放 VLM 的具身能力。

Method: 核心是 Show-Harness 这一"具身 harness"：定义一组紧凑的离散语义动作单元作为 VLM 与机器人之间的共享语言，VLM 直接推理并选择这些单元，本体特定的解释器再确定性地把它们落地为局部机器人动作，从而让 VLM 仍负责细粒度物理决策。基于同一语义动作空间构建 GUMI（GUI 操作接口），把示教采集迁移到 GUI，使人与智能体无需专用遥操作硬件即可跨本体"玩"机器人。实验上同时验证闭源前沿 VLM 零样本控制、以及开源小 VLM 仅数 GPU 小时微调部署。

Result: Show-Harness 加持的 VLM 智能体在任务、本体与环境上表现出稳健泛化，优于代表性的 agentic 与 VLA 范式；并证实无需额外模型容量或昂贵的本体特定预训练即可从基础 VLM 中获得可观的具身能力。

Conclusion: 结论是"接口"设计本身是关键杠杆：通过离散语义动作抽象与确定性本体解释器解耦，可同时复用闭源前沿模型与轻量开源模型，为 VLM 驱动的机器人控制提供低成本、跨本体、易示教的可行路径。

Abstract: Foundation vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit broad intelligence about the world, yet translating this intelligence into robot control remains challenging. We present Show-Harness, an Embodied Harness that enables VLMs to "play" robots through a compact semantic interface linking intent to action. Show-Harness exposes discrete semantic action units that VLMs can naturally reason over, while embodiment-specific interpreters deterministically ground them into local robot actions, keeping the VLM directly responsible for fine-grained physical decisions. Through the same interface, Show-Harness demonstrates the feasibility of (1) directly unlocking closed-source frontier VLMs for zero-shot robot control, and (2) adapting small-scale open-source VLMs for low-cost deployment with just a few GPU-hours of fine-tuning. We further develop GUMI (GUI Manipulation Interface), which extends the same semantic action space to GUI-based demonstration collection, allowing humans and agents to "play" robots across embodiments without specialized teleoperation hardware. Extensive experiments show that Show-Harness-equipped VLM agents generalize robustly across tasks, embodiments, and environments, outperforming representative agentic and VLA paradigms. These results suggest that the right interface can unlock substantial embodied capability from foundation VLMs, without requiring additional model capacity or costly embodiment-specific pretraining.

</details>


### [123] [DCReg: Decoupled Characterization for Efficient Degenerate LiDAR Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.06285)
*Xiangcheng Hu,Xieyuanli Chen,Mingkai Jia,Jin Wu,Ping Tan,Steven L. Waslander*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DCReg提出"检测-表征-缓解"范式：用Schur补将6自由度Hessian解耦为旋转/平移子空间以可靠检测退化，通过基对齐解决特征基歧义实现可解释表征，并用结构化预条件子（特征值截断）进行无损缓解，在长时定位精度提升20-50%、速度提升5-30倍（最高116倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR配准在几何退化环境（如长走廊）中病态：部分运动方向约束弱，导致解不稳定、精度下降。已有"先检测后缓解"方法无法可靠检测、物理解释并在不破坏优化目标的前提下稳定该病态问题。

Method: 三部分：(1) 对Hessian做Schur补分解，将6-DoF配准解耦为3-DoF旋转与平移子空间，消除耦合造成的退化掩蔽；(2) 在子空间内通过基对齐消除特征基歧义，建立特征空间与物理运动方向的稳定映射，给出哪些运动缺约束及程度；(3) 基于谱信息设计结构化预条件子，受MAP正则引导，仅在预条件子内做特征值截断而不改动原问题，保持最小二乘目标与最优解，用预条件共轭梯度优化，仅一个可解释参数。

Result: 在多种环境下，相比退化感知基线，长时定位精度提升20-50%，速度提升5-30倍（最高116倍）。

Conclusion: DCReg通过解耦检测、可解释表征与预条件式缓解，在不破坏最小二乘最优解的前提下有效解决几何退化下的LiDAR配准病态问题，兼顾精度与效率，并开源代码。

Abstract: LiDAR point cloud registration is fundamental to robotic perception and navigation. In geometrically degenerate environments (e.g., corridors), registration becomes ill-conditioned: certain motion directions are weakly constrained, causing unstable solutions and degraded accuracy. Existing detect-then-mitigate methods fail to reliably detect, physically interpret, and stabilize this ill-conditioning without corrupting the optimization. We introduce DCReg (Decoupled Characterization for Ill-conditioned Registration), establishing a detect-characterize-mitigate paradigm that systematically addresses ill-conditioned registration via three innovations. First, DCReg achieves reliable ill-conditioning detection by employing Schur complement decomposition on the Hessian matrix. This decouples the 6-DoF registration into 3-DoF clean rotational and translational subspaces, eliminating coupling effects that mask degeneracy in full-Hessian analyses. Second, within these subspaces, we develop interpretable characterization techniques resolving eigen-basis ambiguities via basis alignment. This establishes stable mappings between eigenspaces and physical motion directions, providing actionable insights on which motions lack constraints and to what extent. Third, leveraging this spectral information, we design a targeted mitigation via a structured preconditioner. Guided by MAP regularization, we implement eigenvalue clamping exclusively within the preconditioner rather than modifying the original problem. This preserves the least-squares objective and minimizer, enabling efficient optimization via Preconditioned Conjugate Gradient with a single interpretable parameter. Experiments demonstrate DCReg achieves 20-50% higher long-duration localization accuracy and 5-30x speedups (up to 116x) over degeneracy-aware baselines across diverse environments. Code: https://github.com/JokerJohn/DCReg

</details>
